## What is a voltage follower and its function?
- It has high input impedance, low output impedance circuit that produces and output voltage similar to the input voltage.
	- $V_{out} = V_{in}$
- It has a gain of 1.
	- $A_{cl} = 1$
- Resolves loading effect issues and allows weak signals to drive loads without degradation.

## Describe how a voltage follower can be built with an Op-Amp
- Connect the output directly to the inverting input.
- Connect the input signal to be followed directly to the non-inverting input.
